# Core freeze progress

## Separation — SEP-06 Native/Clear repeated-scope paired device replay (2026-10-31, focused JVM/API35 TESTED; PARTIAL / HOLD)

- 新增 `WebSkinRecurringScopeParityInstrumentedTest`：同一隔离每日 ALARM 分别通过 Native 月历/编辑器 UI 和真实打包 Clear WebView 执行 `THIS_AND_FUTURE`、`WHOLE_SERIES`。Clear 月历事件使用 Android `SOURCE_TOUCHSCREEN` 注入，可信点击轨迹携带原始键 `2026-10-31T09:00`。Host 取消审核时正式 Store 不变；再次由 Host 确认后保存。两种范围下 Native/Clear 的记录、表单、revision、实例键、身份数、序号水位、草稿、提交及待处理数均相等。
- 定向 JVM `WebSkinRecurrenceBridgeTest`：**13/13 PASS**。API 35 AVD `emulator-5570`、Android 15、WebView `124.0.6367.219`：**1/1 instrumentation method PASS**，内部执行两个范围场景。本次及以后拆分产生两个身份/三条提交；整系列编辑保留一个身份/两条提交。证据 manifest SHA-256 `13C2F83458D467C8FB2D5399E8F4686478632A4D090FC3240F7A1E79E20A7682`；详细日志和测试夹具修正记录位于忽略目录 `.local/android-test/reports/sep06-scope-paired-20261031/`。
- 本次设备配对只覆盖一种 ALARM 和两个范围。已有单次 Clear 与 Native 测试是分开的证据，并非配对回放；其他事件类型、该编辑器在 Studio 的编辑/构建/重导入往返、重复恢复/冲突覆盖、实体/OEM 及 SEP-06 完整等价仍未验证，因此状态保留 **PARTIAL / HOLD**。没有修改生产 Core、Bridge、Clear、Native renderer 或默认皮肤。其他 Agent 未提交的 `WebSkinFormalDeleteInstrumentedTest.kt` 和 `WebSkinRecurrenceInstrumentedTest.kt` 保持原样、未暂存。

## Separation — Native presentation module integrated baseline (historical snapshot 2026-10-30; see the 2026-10-31 runtime recheck below)

- At integrated Git SHA `c93ace28a038f42a5f539a92ae0ec575495a3326`, the Native renderer implementations (timeline, month, memo, AI, settings/navigation, deck/editor, picker, chrome/overlays, and review prompts) are in `:native-skin`; display models and callback slots are in `:skin-api`. `:app` retains Host projections/controller adapters, the Native renderer registry, and protected credential/profile slots. The Native module has no Core, AI, Store, or Android system-service dependency.
- Final serial JVM regression **784/784 PASS, 125 suites, 0 failures/errors/skips**. `verifyNativeSkinBoundary` and `verifySkinSourceBoundary` PASS. AndroidTest Kotlin compilation, Debug APK assembly, and lint PASS (144 actionable tasks). An earlier full run had one Memo integration timeout; it was not reproduced by this final run, and its cause is unknown.
- Final Debug APK: 66,516,562 bytes, SHA-256 `83B78E837330F9D224A4003681F9858004E16DE530E12DE0BC97AE8846C09BE0`. Full JVM log `.local/android-test/reports/native-skin-separation-final-20261030/full-jvm.log`, SHA-256 `3533C0415A71600FFDE277B38B8C63E6EAE50B2D53A55A2F64803768C5BE4766`; Android build log `android-build.log`, SHA-256 `27C1BE88CCB18FBA7DE3221943FB48F39854A617208D457A2C7F606C592135E0`.
- At that snapshot ADB had no online device; Android instrumentation and physical/OEM behavior were **NOT TESTED**. “Clear is currently broken” was an unverified status inherited from prior logs, not a reproduced diagnosis. The two pre-existing WebSkin instrumentation changes remained untouched. The device status below supersedes this snapshot; no full Clear parity, Studio parity, or cutover GO is claimed.

## Separation — Native visual profile is explicit (2026-10-30, slice TESTED; full regression FAIL)

- 当前工作方向按 Owner 最新说明收窄到 Native 呈现分离；本项没有修改 Clear 源码或 `.pcskin`，不代表 Clear 恢复或完整 Native/Skin parity。
- 根因：启动配置虽名为 `native`，实际只清空 renderer registry，让各路由隐式落到原生 fallback；生产配置无法以具名 profile 显式映射十个 surface。
- 新增 Native renderer adapters，分别委托现有 `context.Native()`，没有复制屏幕、持久化、授权或系统操作逻辑；`ActiveVisualSkin.install()` 明确安装该 profile。Deck 保持现有原生单观察者路径，避免 pass-through renderer 同时挂接第二个 controller listener。直接 reset registry 仍可回到既有 fallback，`.pcskin` selection 不受影响。
- `PresentationSkinRegistryTest` + `GlassComposeTest` **22/22 PASS**：覆盖十个唯一 `native/*` renderer ID、registry reset、`.pcskin` selection 独立及 Native profile 下的 Deck 前进导航。API 35 / Android 15 AVD `emulator-5554` 的 `DraftDeckTouchPriorityInstrumentedTest` **2/2 PASS**。`verifySkinSourceBoundary`、`assembleDebug` 与 `lintDebug` PASS；lint 0 errors / 53 warnings。Debug APK SHA-256 `389E3DEAA6A64E94EBE233734AE12EECAC49FF847AA653FD949AEE99F257D257`（67,553,486 bytes）。
- 完整单元回归 **728 tests：727 PASS、1 FAIL**：`WebAiProposalReviewIntegrationTest.memoPagingContinuesTheSortedProjectionWithoutStoreWrites` 导航至 Memo 后 Host state 为 `memoCount=0`，60 秒等待超时。此失败在 Clear Memo 路径；本项未修改 Clear，也不把全量回归记为 PASS。Gradle 日志 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-native-profile-20261030/full-gradle.log` SHA-256 `EF60F6A9BB043D19BA7AA27B1507D717A1B39590E6ED7FE3A7F9CCDAEC0DF07D`；build/lint 日志 SHA-256 `25B4FEBAAD5E4BC6FEAFD45AE6BFECFEA20EC7E4E5E91706388906B9F316EA09`；API 35 instrumentation 日志 SHA-256 `FC97030E65CF4200EEFC08B5C15177E28470040A6357EDDD53CDD0E319D6CFCD`。
- 实现提交 `439eee336903f1b16606b406c5e74734f1f05c0a`（`feat(皮肤分离): 显式注册原生呈现皮肤`）已推送至 `origin/feat/skin-first-separation`。独立验收边界与风险见 [Native visual profile Issue](../issues/PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-NATIVE-VISUAL-PROFILE.md)。
- 实现范围仅为 Native presentation profile 注册与路由，不签核 SEP-01～SEP-16 的功能等价、Studio 可编辑性、WebView/Clear 行为或实体设备。详情见 [Native visual profile Issue](../issues/PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-NATIVE-VISUAL-PROFILE.md)。

## Separation — Android Activity 原生回退 S0-ST-10（2026-10-30，子项 PASS；规格已签核，设备 HOLD）

- 根因：没有从真实 `MainActivity.dispatchTouchEvent` 到 Native Tools 的集成回归；损坏的 `selected` 偏好类型会让 `currentId()` 抛异常；原生回退用异步 `apply()`，调用侧无法确认持久化成功却无条件报成功。Web 皮肤加载失败的安全回退也忽略选择结果。
- 修复：损坏偏好类型回退到 Native；新增同步 Native 选择接口并返回 Boolean；Activity 与 Web 皮肤失败路径根据写入结果呈现成功或“未能确认保存”提示。当前进程仍立即切至 Native，不改 Store。
- 定向测试 **17/17 PASS**：API 35 Robolectric 真实 Activity 触摸分发、单位置序列不触发、第 8 个两位置交替触发并打开 Native Tools、Store 记录/身份/序号/待处理/commit 快照不变；偏好类型损坏恢复；commit 返回 false 或抛异常时报告失败。日志 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-st10-activity-recovery-20261030/targeted-final.log`，SHA-256 `45D4412BFDCCAEEB30967E2EA138EAB8787F2B8DC9F786895F7C159975DD9CDD`。
- 完整离线 Gradle：**677/677 JVM、90 suites、0 failures/errors/skips**；源码边界、Debug APK 和 Lint 通过（0 errors / 53 warnings）。日志 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-st10-activity-recovery-20261030/full-gradle-final.log`，SHA-256 `1770092AE808F228C08A61EDD6BD1C6A0427661B6F5FA333BA2A158F28EC5C58`。APK 67,500,112 bytes，SHA-256 `7E1FCEA722A7FC86F9792794B3560E24F5B448AE1BA2F559D54F0460315222F9`。
- 负责人于 2026-10-30 签核保留两处屏幕位置交替、累计 8 次的回退规则；提示文案改为描述位置，不承诺识别实体手指。Activity 与文案定向回归 2/2 PASS，日志 .local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-st10-gesture-copy-20261030/targeted-final.log，SHA-256 A42062F1E290636B27C21A72792944FE94A5185EAEEF739CBFA1603329ECBD22；文案提交 1f5faa3 已推送。实际设备触摸、TalkBack/无障碍和冷进程恢复仍 NOT TESTED，S0-ST-10 保持 PARTIAL / HOLD。

## Separation — Clear 系统日历导入 `S0-ST-11`（2026-10-30，Host/Store 自动化 PASS；Android 设备 HOLD）

- 根因：Clear 的 `calendar.import.system` 权限/动作路由实际已经存在，但 parity matrix 的“没有对应命令”描述过时。导入批次只在完整循环正常结束时调用 `finishMigration()`；单条写入异常会留下开放批次。`Rejected` 结果被忽略，查询起止日期也分别读取当前日，存在跨本地午夜时窗口不一致的窄竞态。
- 修复：创建批次时在 IO block 内同步捕获 ID，确保取消与 dispatcher 返回竞态下仍进入收尾；创建成功后在 `NonCancellable` finalizer 结束批次并保留警告。部分成功失败后刷新已提交事件并协调提醒。拒绝的行记录原因并向用户显示计数；重试沿用 `(source, externalId)` 幂等键。系统日历查询现在只捕获一次本地日期。
- 定向回归 **32/32 PASS**，覆盖 Provider 范围/字段/稳定 ID、权限拒绝无查询和无写入、批次创建边界取消、AI 锁竞争拒绝、中途 SQLite 失败、批次结束、部分成功安全重试、重复导入及提醒意图。修复前同一 Host 用例在等待批次完成时 20 秒超时；原始首次控制台未单独留档。最终定向日志 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-st11-system-import-20261030/targeted-final.log`，SHA-256 `E5D821051DD359FF7ECFEC0E4A7141EEDF00DCBB25DEFFC73B27D3D5E10AA9C7`。
- 最终完整离线 Gradle **673/673 JVM、89 suites、0 failures/errors/skips**；源码边界、Debug APK、lint 均 PASS。日志 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-st11-system-import-20261030/full-gradle-final-r3.log`，SHA-256 `4E7E3FAD9020986360E495C1D2670D766E5103C1BEB033ACD54D4AEC415A2095`。APK 67,498,300 bytes，SHA-256 `DBD9E1844510F6BFCD9C24880F4CB2D4A86ED05F11DD34FD2C04DEDBD752A221`。
- `adb devices -l` 无在线设备。Android WebView、实际 Provider/权限弹窗与撤销流程 **NOT TESTED**；系统日历源提醒是否导入仍无明确合同，且显式 `ALL_DAY` 精度未覆盖。Matrix 状态 **PARTIAL / HOLD**，不据模拟测试签核设备 parity。详见 [独立 Issue](../issues/PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-SYSTEM-CALENDAR-IMPORT.md)。
- 实现提交并推送：`a0e4353` (`fix(分离): 补齐系统日历导入失败恢复`)。

## Separation — Clear 旧版数据库迁移 `S0-ST-12`（2026-10-30，Host/Preview 自动化 PASS；Android WebView/设备 HOLD）

- 根因：旧数据库存在但缺少 `events` 表时，`LegacyImporter` 创建批次后提前返回，未写入结束时间；重复尝试持续留下未完成批次。Host 的 `SemanticOptions` 未注册旧版迁移动作，因此 Clear 无法到达已有原生确认流程。
- 修复：缺表分支把警告写入并结束迁移批次；Host 增加闭合选项 `data.legacy.migrate`，并复用 Android 原生确认对话框；Studio Preview 增加非写入选项与 Mock 轨迹。旧数据库仍只读、保留，`v01:<legacyId>` 来源映射及幂等机制不变。
- 修复前红测证实缺表连续运行后迁移批次未结束（预期 `(1,0)`，实际 `(1,1)`）。定向回归 **28/28 PASS**：`LegacyMigrationTest` 4、Bridge 4、Host 投影 19、PowerCalApp Robolectric/Compose 集成 1。集成验证取消无写入、确认后只读导入隔离 fixture、旧文件 SHA 不变、重试不增加事件/身份/序号。红测/定向日志及哈希见 [独立 Issue](../issues/PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-LEGACY-MIGRATION.md)。
- 最终完整离线 Gradle **666/666 JVM、88 suites、0 failures/errors/skips、88/88 tasks PASS**；源码边界、Debug 与 AndroidTest APK、lint 成功（0 errors / 52 warnings）。最终日志 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-st12-migration-20261030/full-gradle-final.log`，SHA-256 `8749EABA40B6BCBF762E564857D9BEB5472F9FF6652F0BBD9D7A58858399206F`。Debug APK SHA-256 `4C516E4067F2C74FFF05F927CB469B23E137B587EAD12A66EABF76927D6DB61D`；AndroidTest APK SHA-256 `C23375D347A00880431B9A2807E0BFAB005973D690A8FF8BECC4DF94B6122931`。
- Skin Studio Python **61/61 PASS**；Chrome Headless/CDP Preview 从 Clear 月历“导入选项”点击迁移项，Mock 收到动作并断言无数据库读写/删除，**PASS（Mock）**。canonical Clear 9 files / 392,170 bytes；源码、checked-in skin、APK 中资源 SHA-256 均为 `af640cbca89a870c2d1ea733eb562ed35e6082b75b08fe7aa37afa9d4ff6f98a`。证据均在 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-st12-migration-20261030/`。
- `adb devices -l` 没有在线设备，AndroidTest APK 仅构建，生产 Android WebView、设备迁移与旧闹钟 handoff **NOT TESTED**。S0-ST-12 保持 **PARTIAL / HOLD**，不签核整体 parity/GO；原生确认继续提示用户检查旧时钟闹钟。独立报告：[PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-LEGACY-MIGRATION](../issues/PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-LEGACY-MIGRATION.md)。实现提交并推送：`2a2cb7ab7bf500d761f0594c97ee4ede797cbd7b`（`fix(皮肤分离): 接入旧版数据迁移并收尾失败批次`）。

### Follow-up — S0-ST-12 migration poller SQLite BUSY (2026-10-30)

- A forced full run found the read-only status poll in LegacyHostMigrationTest could overlap an active writer transaction; SQLITE_BUSY escaped the test poller. The fix retries only SQLiteDatabaseLockedException within the existing deadline; unexpected failures still fail. Production LegacyImporter was unchanged.

- Later full offline Gradle **727/727 JVM, 100 suites, 0 failures/errors/skips; 88/88 tasks PASS; lint 0 errors / 53 warnings**; log SHA-256 68D8D1CFFDCA45F152608A026E81D39C54A89C58A3EBDBC11E7947BC30A278FB. Commit b913839 is pushed. Actual Android migration WebView/SAF and old-alarm handoff remain NOT TESTED; S0-ST-12 stays PARTIAL / HOLD.

## Separation — Clear AI 聊天历史清空与回退 `S0-AI-01` / `S0-AI-04`（2026-10-29，PARTIAL / HOLD）

- 根因：Native 历史存于 `AiChatHistoryStore` 且已有清空/回退语义；Clear 只显示最近 40 条角色与文本，没有稳定消息 ID、清空/回退动作或安全 prefill 通知，因此只能改 DOM，不能修改 Host 持有的历史。
- 修复：新增纯 Host 历史 reducer、稳定 Host 消息 ID、revision fencing 和 closed-schema `clearChat` / `rewindChat`；用户编辑重发/撤回移除所选用户消息及后续轮次，助手回退保留该回复。空历史清空及最后助手回复回退为 no-op。Host 只有真实历史变化时才更新 revision/持久化；只有“编辑并重发”会替换 composer，清空/撤回/助手回退保留未发送文本；普通状态重绘也不覆盖用户输入。历史动作不接触正式事件、草稿、提交记录或身份计数；正式/新建事件编辑与草稿卡组活跃时 Host 禁止历史修改。Clear 增加无障碍控件；Studio Mock 使用非写入、版本校验的模拟。

## Separation — Clear AI 批量操作桥接 `S0-AI-04a`（2026-10-29，PARTIAL / HOLD）

- 根因：Clear/Studio 之前缺少 AI 批量范围选择、状态回传和停止/恢复入口。复核还发现强制恢复可误解锁正常 RUNNING 批次，且释放报告会把未知计数显示成 0；待审 AI 提案与批次范围选择之间也缺少完整互斥。Native 回退聊天页启动范围选择后没有就地取消入口。
- 修复：Clear 只发起 Host 范围选择、请求停止或请求恢复；Host/Store 继续拥有版本快照、审核授权和写入。强制恢复只允许 `CANCEL_REQUESTED` 且心跳过期至少 10 分钟，并在事务内核对精确 session、epoch、当前锁 epoch、状态和时间。释放结果投影为 `outcomeKnown=false` 且不输出计数。AI 提案审核和批次选择互斥；Studio Mock 具有“已取消但超时”的独立夹具，只在其已知的部分结果中显示 1 发布/2 保留。Native 回退页增加取消范围选择入口，批次期间禁用清聊天/缓存；此原生 UI 尚未 Android 验证。

- 根因：Clear 时间轴只有打开事件的点击目标；Host Bridge 没有安全移动命令，皮肤不能请求移动正式事件。新增封闭 schema `timeline.move`，Host 从当前时间轴投影、相机与 zone 校验事件及 revision，以 Native `TimelineCoordinate.snap` 计算目标，只创建一张带来源 revision 栅栏的 `REPLACE` 草稿并打开现有 Deck；正式事件保持不变，用户仍须审核/发布。重复事件、Memo、纯日期 Deadline、过期投影、锁定操作及无效日期均拒绝或返回状态。Native 拖动也传递来源 revision，`PowerStore.newDraft` 在同一事务内检查可选 `expectedRevision`，防止读取后版本竞态。
- Clear 保留普通点击编辑；500ms 长按后拖动，阈值前移动会取消。Studio 新增 `timeline-drag` 非写入 Mock 情景。Chrome 156 CDP 真实指针回归：普通点击只打开编辑器；拖动将预览替换草稿从 `2026-10-22 09:00` 移到 `12:00`，一小时持续时间保留，正式时间毫秒仍为 `1792630800000`；重复事件触摸拖动被拒绝且没有候选草稿。三条路径 **3/3 PASS**。截图 SHA-256 `7666D6D19E5A0D3C5258AA5677F7B3EAA57150CBBEC109715E92B694A0DA496C`；浏览器 JSON、CDP 日志和代码见独立报告。
- 定向 `WebSkinEditBridgeTest` + `PowerStoreTest` **116/116 PASS**；Skin Studio **59/59 PASS**；JS `node --check` 4/4；规范 Clear 包 9 files / 384,912 bytes，SHA-256 `D0D136EEF118DC52CE767E8416EC1BD3B3134F75302BC64C3D3ED4FCA5192BF9`；source boundary 与独立 `lintDebug` PASS（52 warnings / 0 errors）；Debug/AndroidTest APK 均构建成功，哈希见 Issue 报告。
- 标准默认 worker 的完整离线 Gradle JVM 门禁两次都因已有 `WebAiProposalReviewIntegrationTest.memoPagingContinuesTheSortedProjectionWithoutStoreWrites` 60 秒等待超时而 FAIL（619 tests / 1 failure）。该测试单独运行 PASS；同一干净父提交 `057b39e` 的全量基线也为 613 tests / 同一失败。随后串行完整门禁 `--max-workers=1` **BUILD SUCCESSFUL，619/619 tests、84 suites、0 failures/errors/skips、88/88 tasks**，source boundary、Debug/AndroidTest APK 与 lint 通过（0 errors / 52 warnings）。因此全量功能回归已有串行 PASS，但默认并行的既有 Memo 测试稳定性问题仍须保留记录。`emulator-5582` 当时已在线且所有权不明，未触碰；Android WebView/AVD 和实体设备 **NOT TESTED**。S0-TL-04 与父项 `SEP-01` 仍不是完整 parity PASS / cutover GO。
- 独立报告：[PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-CLEAR-TIMELINE-DRAG](../issues/PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-CLEAR-TIMELINE-DRAG.md)。串行完整门禁日志 SHA-256 `EE72E74A2015EC85E414795A684797285197E5B259BAE8CD3BC2699078AA2652`；JVM JUnit XML 84 suites 保存在 `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-timeline-drag/full-junit/`。第二次默认 worker 失败日志 SHA-256 `A839981EEA3B5CC7BA3B35C567B9AF5AFE5792063D71E7F1E41020C4F2C4ADBC`；基线对照日志 SHA-256 `F3E6DD815899CFE55896F676E0B1E07F7BBF236D71D86822DBBF4DA5708495C6`。
- 实现、回归、矩阵与独立 Issue 已提交 `1d78353e0866c7bc19c6a555fb65cbc454aea1f2`；本条同时回填该 SHA。下一项从重建矩阵按风险选择；整体 Skin-First separation 仍 **IN PROGRESS**。

## Separation — SEP-01 Clear long-press drag / zoom-reset touch follow-up（2026-10-30，窄项 API 35 TESTED；整行仍 NOT TESTED）

## Separation — Clear timeline measured-title greedy folding `S0-TL-05` (2026-10-29, PARTIAL)

- Root cause: Clear had no measured-label collision policy; labels rendered with event controls and could not fold by actual visible text geometry. Added deterministic `title-folding.js` pure strategy (screen Y/event sequence/stable ID order, strict rectangle overlap, hidden candidates never replace lead), DOM `Range` measurement, unified viewport coordinates with left/right clipping, one-line lane-width ellipsis, Activity start anchoring when its true visual frame is too short, and reflow after scroll/resize/render/font readiness. Only labels hide; event geometry, event buttons, complete accessible names and date-only Deadline strip remain. No `×N`.
- Evidence: Node pure function **10/10 PASS**; Skin Studio Python **59/59 PASS**; JS syntax, deterministic Clear bundle and v3 validation PASS. Edge 156 Studio Mock at 1460×960 retained 28/28 events and unchanged geometry; measured A/B and C/D text collisions fold correctly although event times do not overlap; left/right horizontal clipping and restoration, vertical scroll, zoom/reset/date reset, folded-event pointer hit and accessibility checks PASS. Trace, screenshots, package/test logs and SHA-256 are in the independent Issue report and ignored `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-greedy-title-folding/run-20261029-16/` evidence directory.
- Follow-up at code tree `1d78353e0866c7bc19c6a555fb65cbc454aea1f2`: pure greedy strategy **10/10 PASS**, Clear canonical package verify PASS (9 files / 384,912 bytes), and the serialized full offline Gradle gate **619/619 JVM, 84 suites, 88/88 tasks PASS**. The earlier API 36 SDK BLOCKED result is superseded for compile/test/APK/lint only. Android WebView/AVD, physical/OEM and matched Native/Clear parity remain **NOT TESTED**; full `S0-TL-05` parity remains NOT TESTED, not release/cutover GO. Follow-up logs and APK hashes are in the independent Issue report and `.local/android-test/reports/separation-s0-timeline-drag/`.
- 实现、回归、独立 Issue 与矩阵/进度窄项已提交 `c6b6f7d10e068ee0d4d292505f643b0fab4d1164`（`feat(分离): 实现 Clear 时间轴标题贪心折叠`）；完整 SHA 回填由后续独立文档提交记录。整体 Skin-First separation 继续 IN PROGRESS。

### Follow-up — API 35 Clear scroll/zoom reflow（2026-10-30）

- 在真实 `PowerCalApp` + Clear WebView 上扩展横向滚动/裁切/恢复、纵向滚动/恢复、16h 缩放和 24h 复位；AndroidJUnitRunner 类 **2/2 PASS**。所有阶段都按实际测量矩形重算并维持 `A visible → B folded against A → C visible compared with A → D folded against C`；恢复后 rectangles 与 baseline 差在 1 CSS px 内，四个事件命中目标保留。API 35 / Android 15 / WebView `124.0.6367.219` / `emulator-5582`。
- 缩放实测另外定位 `S0-TL-01` Host 发布缺陷：action 更新 `hours` 后，同步 projection 使用旧 composition 捕获的 `camera`，导致 24h stale state。`PowerCalApp.kt` 现在在发布时由 live `anchorMillis` + clamp 后 `hours` 构造投影相机；真实触摸验证 Host 与 Clear 从 24→16→24h。该根因独立记入 `PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-CLEAR-TIMELINE-GEOMETRY`。
- 强制 JVM **727/727 / 100 suites / 0 failures/errors/skips**；source-boundary/JVM/Debug/lint Gradle gate BUILD SUCCESSFUL，lint 0 errors / 53 warnings；AndroidTest rebuild 53/53 tasks，Skin Studio Python 61/61，纯策略 10/10，Clear canonical package 9 files / 394,086 bytes PASS。详细环境、矩形、APK 与日志 hashes 见两个独立 Issue 报告；trace SHA-256 `4929252FA8A73B6F1D84D1158815AD0EF26CA1F0130FE6605A25EDCA7378E0AC`，runner SHA-256 `438972A22823E7E17A1E16A469CD02D7A33C2C39B90433E99060B61217DC5758`。
- 代码/AndroidTest commit `2c4f9afe2d91c99fb016ec09d2702444be4c6f30`（`fix(分离): 发布 Clear 时间轴缩放状态并补充实测`）已推送。API35 测试窄项已签核；TalkBack、实体/OEM 与完整跨皮肤 camera/scroll/zoom parity 仍 NOT TESTED，`S0-TL-05` 总体 PARTIAL / HOLD。

## Separation — v3 Manifest 扩展元数据兼容（2026-10-29）

- 关闭 `S0-PKG-04` 的窄兼容缺陷：Studio v3 解析器此前拒绝未知顶层 Manifest 字段，而 Android 接受并忽略这些字段。现将未知字段定义为不授予权限的 opaque metadata，Studio 导入/编辑/导出保留其值；`capabilities` 仍为封闭白名单，非法权限仍拒绝。

## Separation — AI proposal review initial vertical slice (2026-10-27)

- Clear v3 now receives a bounded Host `aiProposalReview` projection and can send closed-schema `resolveAiProposal` actions for publish, stage, reject, or supplement. Formal publication waits for a trusted Compose Host review of the full before/after snapshot; the Host rechecks target snapshots/revisions before invoking the existing `AiDraftService`. Cancellation preserves the review and writes nothing. Skin JS receives no approval token.

- Full offline gate `:app:verifySkinSourceBoundary :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :app:assembleDebugAndroidTest :app:lintDebug --offline --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain`: **582/582 JVM tests / 82 suites / 0 failures, errors or skips / 88 tasks PASS**; lint 0 errors / 52 warnings. ## Separation — S0-ST-17 legacy flat v2 explicit restore consent (2026-10-30)

- Contract source: `STABLE_DATA_CONTRACT_V1.md` §4. The old flat v2 file has neither payload SHA-256 nor `storeLineageId`; accepting it can only mean the user accepts the unverified file risk. It cannot carry a source lineage into the restored DB. Core creates a fresh UUID lineage for the destination.
- Production path was already present in `PowerCalApp`/`BackupEnvelope`: the first Host restore attempt rejects flat v2, retains its text only in memory and shows a warning; cancel writes nothing; the explicit “仍要恢复” action passes `allowLegacy=true` into the same empty-target transactional restore. The remaining issue was absence of Host UI integration evidence, not a missing production branch.
- Added two API 35 Robolectric tests to `BackupRestoreHostTest`. They use random isolated source/target DBs. One proves the warning and cancel leave records/drafts/identities/sequence/commits/reminder snapshot unchanged; the other confirms restore, preserved event/draft data, unverified status and a new target lineage distinct from both source Envelope and pre-restore target.
- `BackupRestoreHostTest` **9/9 PASS**; related Host/Bridge/projection/export suites **41/41 PASS**. Full offline boundary/JVM/Debug/AndroidTest assembly/Lint gate **699/699 JVM tests / 93 suites / 0 failures/errors/skips**, 88 actionable tasks (14 executed / 74 up-to-date); lint 0 errors / 53 issues. Full `--rerun-tasks` test pass also confirmed 699/699, all 32 tasks executed. The no-write comparison includes the destination lineage UUID.

## Separation — S0-ST-19 exact alarm settings and permission recovery (2026-10-30)

- Root causes: Clear had no v3 exact-alarm status/action; `BLOCKED` reminders were not eligible for `dueIntents()` after grant; the SecurityException path did not distinguish a revoked permission; and ON_RESUME did not force a Bridge snapshot publication when OS permission state changed without other Compose state changes.
- Added a Settings-only, API 31+ closed-schema Bridge action and exact-alarm status projection. Reconciliation requeues only permission-blocked EXACT intents; SecurityException handling rereads access and uses the retryable hint only when access is actually missing. ON_RESUME increments the Bridge revision after reconciliation/reload. Clear and Studio Mock now expose and exercise the action. API 35 Robolectric/Compose verifies system Intent URI/flags, denied/granted return paths, state-revision advance and unchanged formal record/commit/identity/sequence snapshots; a BootReceiver test verifies the permission-change broadcast recovers BLOCKED→SCHEDULED.

## Separation — S0-ST-20 manual reminder reconciliation (2026-10-30)

- Root cause: Native `onReconcile` always displayed success and did not catch/report errors; Clear had no v3 manual-reconcile action or result projection. The scheduler's ordinary pass processes at most 400 due/scheduled and 100 cancelled rows, so reusing that pass for an explicit “reconcile all” action could falsely imply completion beyond those limits.
- Added a Settings-only closed-schema `reconcileReminders` Host action and accessible Clear status/button. Native Tools and Clear now share running/success/blocked/error feedback. Explicit manual recovery walks stable keyset pages for all due/scheduled and cancelled intents; lifecycle/startup reconciliation retains its bounded one-pass behavior.
- API 35 Robolectric/Compose: 3 new integration tests cover permission-blocked→scheduled recovery, Store read failure without false success, and 405 isolated orphan intents crossing the 400/100 legacy batch limits. Formal commit, identity and sequence snapshots remain unchanged. Together with Bridge, exact-settings, scheduler-validation and BootReceiver suites: **31/31 PASS**.

## Separation — S0-ED-02 transient editor save and lock recovery (2026-10-30)

- Audit found a gap in `WebSkinEditService.requestCommitReview()`: transient `NewEventEditSession` saves did not precheck `PowerStore.isLocked()`, unlike create and persisted-deck paths. Complete forms requested trusted review before the Store rejected the locked write; incomplete forms mapped the lock exception to generic `failed`. Forms stayed in memory, but the retryable lock state was inconsistent.
- Added Store-lock preflight for meaningful transient saves and lock-aware mapping for a lock that arrives after preflight. A blocked save now returns `locked`, retains the in-memory form, and creates no review, draft, formal record, identity, sequence or queue order. Store transactions remain authoritative. Added regressions for incomplete MEMO retry, complete ALARM publish retry, and all four kinds staging incomplete content then publishing the same identity only after Host review.
- `WebSkinEditBridgeTest` **35/35 PASS**. ## Separation — S0-ED-03 editor lifecycle checkpoint and Activity recreation (2026-10-30)

- Read-only trace found Native new-editor open is memory-only; the `PowerCalApp` `ON_STOP` observer dispatches transient-session checkpoint to process `appScope`, while `LifecycleCheckpointGate` suppresses same-cycle repeats and resets at `ON_START`. Web Bridge checkpoint and skin-change checkpoint are separate paths. No deterministic production defect was found; the missing evidence was an integration test through the actual `PowerCalApp` lifecycle observer.
- Added API 35 Robolectric `PowerCalAppLifecycleCheckpointTest`: clean MEMO stop leaves Store counters untouched; changed ALARM stop writes exactly one draft and repeated stop/start preserves its identity/version/order; actual `Activity.recreate()` destroys the old Activity, and closing/reopening the isolated Store recovers the same modified MEMO draft without creating a formal event or commit. Test explicitly restores shared preferences and uses a unique isolated database. No production code changed.

## Separation — S0-ED-04 formal editor revision conflicts and recovery (2026-10-30)

- Read-only audit found Core already fences formal writes by editor lease and expected revision. Existing tests covered related delete review conflicts and type conversion, but did not directly prove stale revision handling for ordinary `EditSession.save()` or a Web `SaveEvent` whose Host review remained open while another versioned write committed. Durable formal-editor checkpoint recovery after Store reopen also lacked a direct regression. No production defect was reproduced; the gap was test evidence.
- Added three regressions: ordinary formal edit preserves its in-memory proposal and concurrent completion state on revision conflict; a checkpointed formal Activity edit resumes after closing/reopening the isolated Store and saves once to the same identity; Web Host review rejects a changed revision, preserves the proposal and replays as conflict. No production source changed.
- Forced focused run: `PowerStoreTest` 100/100, `WebSkinEditBridgeTest` 36/36, `DirectEventEditorComposeTest` 6/6; **142/142**, 0 failures/errors/skips, 32 tasks executed. ## Separation — S0-ED-05 formal deletion confirmation and recurrence semantics (2026-10-30)

- Read-only audit confirmed the Host binds a formal delete review to the active session, current full record and form snapshot; approval rechecks these before calling `EditSession.delete()`, and Store transaction rechecks the editor lease and expected revision. Existing JVM tests covered non-recurring confirmation/cancel/idempotence and a changed-revision conflict. The API 35 instrumentation source covered recurring Activity cancel/approve but had not produced a device result. No production defect was reproduced; direct JVM coverage of deleting a whole recurring series was missing.
- Added `formalRecurringDeleteCancelsTheWholeSeriesAndDiscardsItsPendingEdit`: cancel leaves the series, recurrence rule, pending replacement, identity and commit count unchanged; approved retry tombstones the same identity, preserves the recurrence rule in the tombstone, discards the pending edit, creates one commit, and replays idempotently. Production code unchanged.

- Reproduced on the API 35 AVD: Clear loaded Settings and opened the real Android `READ_CALENDAR` dialog, but denying it produced no visible Clear feedback. Root cause: `PowerCalApp.message` was shown only through Compose while Clear uses a full-canvas WebView; the Bridge state and Clear renderer had no host-notice projection.
- Added transient read-only `hostNotice` to the existing state frame and rendered it in Clear as visible, accessible status text. Existing Host timeout still clears it after five seconds. No action, Store write, or permission behavior changed. Canonical Studio source rebuilt the bundled `.pcskin`; Studio source remains editable.
- API 35 / Android 15 / WebView 124 / `emulator-5570`: the real Clear WebView → Host option → Android permission prompt was exercised for Deny and Allow against an empty system Calendar Provider. - Skin Studio Python **63/63 PASS**; Chrome/CDP Bridge notice source replay **PASS**; `node --check` and canonical `.pcskin` verification **PASS**. Full offline Gradle: **784/784 JVM / 125 suites / 0 failures, errors or skips; BUILD SUCCESSFUL; lint 0 errors / 55 warnings**. Debug APK SHA-256 `B4BEB1F562D416D047663BD35C520C4DC691C27530C15187AA69794D91DE9A61`; AndroidTest APK SHA-256 `3C5181CC8A0D914820AE02B289C25865452F9CD9A18465BE3FAE37634A756831`.
- Runtime/test/contract commit `f2bc056ead891b631a66a56a6c1a73100ce00d2b` (`fix(皮肤分离): 显示Clear宿主状态通知`) is pushed to `origin/feat/skin-first-separation`. ## Separation — SEP-07 API 35 deletion conflict and tombstone recovery (2026-10-31)

- API 35 / Android 15 / WebView 124 / `emulator-5570`: Clear formal delete was re-opened with an independent Store writer committing a newer revision while Host confirmation was pending. Approval of the stale delete was rejected; the newer formal record and identity remained active, no delete commit or pending draft was added, and the user could close the clean stale editor to refresh the Clear timeline. Clear also deleted a formal event, opened its tombstone recovery proposal, verified Host cancellation left the tombstone intact, then retried Host approval and restored the same eventSeq at revision +2 without advancing the sequence watermark.
- `WebSkinFormalDeleteTouchInstrumentedTest`: touchscreen delete cancel/approve + stale revision conflict/recovery + tombstone restore **3/3 PASS**. Direct instrumentation log: `.local/android-test/reports/sep07-delete-conflict-20261031/final-device-class-with-restore.log`. Complete API 35 device traces and hashes are in the report directory. A JVM test checks clean stale close without a write; dirty stale editor input remains preserved and cannot silently close.
- Root fix: `CloseEvent` previously checkpointed every editor, so an unchanged form bound to an old revision could never exit. Added a read-only `EditSession.formalVersionIsCurrent()` check; only a clean stale session bypasses checkpoint and releases its lease. Dirty sessions retain the existing checkpoint rejection. Clear projection reload is limited to `CloseEvent`, not transient `CloseDeck`.
- Full offline Android gate: **784/784 JVM / 125 suites / zero failures/errors/skips / 171/171 tasks**, source boundaries, Debug and AndroidTest APK assembly, Lint **0 errors / 55 warnings**. Skin Studio **63/63 PASS**. SEP-07 remains **PARTIAL / HOLD / INCOMPLETE** for TalkBack, matched Native/Clear replay and physical/OEM evidence. The independent uncommitted edits in `WebSkinFormalDeleteInstrumentedTest.kt` and `WebSkinRecurrenceInstrumentedTest.kt` remain untouched and unstaged. See [formal-delete Issue](../issues/PC-ISSUE-SEPARATION-WEB-FORMAL-DELETE.md).

### SEP-16 Clear Host-review competing-version rejection (2026-10-31)

## SEP-01 / S0-TL-01 independent Native/Clear wing touch and startup clipping (2026-10-31)

- Four same-time events per side reproduced the Native left-wing startup defect: lane 0 had an empty accessibility bounds rect while the scroll range was `0..458 px`. `Int.MAX_VALUE` was supplied before persisted events loaded; the first measure treated it as the unbounded `ScrollState.maxValue` sentinel and clamped the requested offset to zero. The fix waits until the actual positive finite range appears, then scrolls once; subsequent recomposition/restoration does not snap over user scrolling.
- On API 35 / Android 15 / WebView 124, `emulator-5570`, the paired test used actual touchscreen swipes on both Native and Clear left/right wings. Native left initializes `458/458 px` and right `0/458`; the left event moves from screen x=267 to x=419 while the right wing stays still. Clear left initializes `164.19/164 CSS px` and right `0/164`; right event x=304.10→242.38 after right swipe, and left event x=111.81→173.52 after left swipe; the opposite wing remains unchanged. Host camera stays at 24 hours and both isolated Stores keep zero drafts/commits. Focused pair **1/1 PASS**; full viewport class **4/4 PASS**.
- Commit `cd9e7bef39a1d8ea59ca0f4ea8e2ed99e3037f67` is pushed to `origin/feat/skin-first-separation`. Its clean source archive SHA-256 is `A45EFD9E9528216872C96F312C5667D2C55E6BC81E307FC7AD214BCF1D9F169A` (tree `09fb277b5a2746aa23cf75d9b706949784f7a2fc`). Full offline Gradle gate: **784/784 JVM / 125 suites / 0 failures/errors/skips; 172 tasks**, boundaries, Debug/AndroidTest APK, lint and device class passed. Skin Studio Python **63/63 PASS**. Gate log SHA-256 `AA35DF59C551F2CF7A99EB32D37AF632EEA33FC6D91F564197D62DA36A507118`; Studio log `74F1444666072FC604EE8C45D45AC32504338B548273F9587DE2C3D065A19858`; device class log `9BA3325A24CD069F708CC8F041F7DD2AA13BC9881591648FAEF9D8C5CAD106AA`; measured geometry trace `121C88624BBE61DB1916ED78F9A8AAEEDCE2DE9B8E7BE1DABECAE765ACBF6C96`. Debug / AndroidTest APK SHA-256: `83C9EDAD5DBE47F3D78C5E9BC35435CAD59C674FBF79D66C6054A982C51D767D` / `751D17198EFE50E9203B3A87737271C28A06ED59410E328F5553C8FE924C62F2`. All artifacts are under `.local/android-test/reports/sep01-native-clear-wings-parity-20261031/`.

Independent wing scroll and Native startup clipping are now evidenced slices; overall `S0-TL-01` / `SEP-01` remain **PARTIAL / HOLD** for remaining gesture, reset-touch, accessibility and physical/OEM checks. ## SEP-01 / S0-TL-01 Clear single-finger vertical camera pan parity (2026-10-31)

- 修复确认的实际差异：Clear 原先没有垂直相机拖动，只能通过 ±180 分钟按钮平移；新建页内触摸无法抵达任意相机位置。现在 Clear 在空白时间轴区域接收可信单指纵向触摸，将位移按当前视窗小时数/高度换算成分钟并调用既有 panTimeline；交互控件与事件卡跳过，横向泳道仍由浏览器处理。无 Core/Host/Bridge 修改。
- API 35 / Android 15 / WebView 124 / emulator-5570：Native 与 Clear 均由 local noon/16h 通过真实 Android 触控上移 180 分钟到 15:00/16h，Host 相机完全相同。Clear 指针 down/move/up 均可信、8 个移动、无 pointercancel、axisScroll.scrollTop 不变；两 Store 均 0 draft/0 commit。配对用例 1/1 PASS；完整 TimelineReturnViewportInstrumentedTest 5/5 PASS（含 Native/Clear 左右独立翼滚动、今日/缩放/复位和返回视口）。API 35 注入的 SOURCE_TOUCHSCREEN 在 WebView 报告空 pointerType，运行时仅对可信 primary 左键事件接受此兼容值。首次 45 分钟样本未越过 Native touch slop，第二次为错误地限定 WebView pointerType 的测试断言；修正后的最终触控配对通过。
- node --check、Skin Studio Python 65/65、Clear canonical bundle 与 Debug APK 比对均通过。强制串行离线完整门禁 785/785 JVM / 125 suites / 0 failures/errors/skips / 171 tasks PASS；Skin/Native source boundary、Debug/AndroidTest APK 与 Lint 均通过（0 errors/55 warnings）。门禁 SHA-256 77A6D4588776B85FCA7CB49C77BE66DDBB7A5885BE1502CC83388C399FE57DD1；APK 与设备/Studio证据见现有几何 Issue 的 follow-up。
